: '该用户名已被注册' };
    }

    // 首位注册用户自动赋予管理员角色
    const isFirst = users.length === 0;
    const roleId = isFirst ? 'role-admin' : 'role-operator';
    const now = nowISO();
    const newUser: User = {
      id: genId(),
      username: name,
      realName: realName?.trim() || (isFirst ? '系统管理员' : name),
      password: p,
      roleId,
      status: 'active',
      createdAt: now,
      lastLoginAt: now,
    };

    setUsers((prev) => [...prev, newUser]);
    writeJSON(SESSION_KEY, newUser.id);
    setCurrentUserId(newUser.id);
    return { ok: true };
  };

  const logout = () => {
    writeJSON(SESSION_KEY, null);
    setCurrentUserId(null);
  };

  // ---- 用户 CRUD ----
  const addUser: AuthContextType['addUser'] = (data) => {
    const name = data.username.trim();
    if (name.length < 2) return { ok: false, error: '用户名至少 2 个字符' };
    if (data.password.length < 4) return { ok: false, error: '密码至少 4 个字符' };
    if (users.some((x) => x.username.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase())) {
      return { ok: false, error: '用户名已存在' };
    }
    const newUser: User = {
      id: genId(),
      username: name,
      realName: data.realName.trim() || name,
      password: data.password,
      roleId: data.roleId || 'role-operator',
      status: 'active',
      createdAt: nowISO(),
    };
    setUsers((prev) => [...prev, newUser]);
    return { ok: true };
  };

  const updateUser: AuthContextType['updateUser'] = (id, data) => {
    if (data.username) {
      const name = data.username.trim();
      if (users.some((x) => x.id !== id && x.username.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase())) {
        return { ok: false, error: '该用户名已被占用' };
      }
    }
    setUsers((prev) =>
      prev.map((u) => {
        if (u.id !== id) return u;
        return {
          ...u,
          ...data,
          username: data.username ? data.username.trim() : u.username,
          realName: data.realName !== undefined ? data.realName.trim() : u.realName,
        };
      }),
    );
    return { ok: true };
  };

  const deleteUser: AuthContextType['deleteUser'] = (id) => {
    const target = users.find((u) => u.id === id);
    if (!target) return { ok: false, error: '用户不存在' };
    if (target.id === currentUserId) return { ok: false, error: '无法删除当前正在登录的账号' };
    if (target.username === 'admin') return { ok: false, error: '系统初始管理员账号不可删除' };
    setUsers((prev) => prev.filter((u) => u.id !== id));
    return { ok: true };
  };

  const resetUserPassword: AuthContextType['resetUserPassword'] = (id, newPassword) => {
    const pwd = newPassword || '123456';
    setUsers((prev) =>
      prev.map((u) => (u.id === id ? { ...u, password: pwd } : u)),
    );
    return { ok: true, password: pwd };
  };

  const toggleUserStatus: AuthContextType['toggleUserStatus'] = (id) => {
    const target = users.find((u) => u.id === id);
    if (!target) return { ok: false, error: '用户不存在' };
    if (target.id === currentUserId) return { ok: false, error: '无法禁用当前正在登录的账号' };
    if (target.username === 'admin') return { ok: false, error: '系统管理员账号不可禁用' };

    const nextStatus = target.status === 'active' ? 'disabled' : 'active';
    setUsers((prev) =>
      prev.map((u) => (u.id === id ? { ...u, status: nextStatus } : u)),
    );
    return { ok: true };
  };

  // ---- 角色 CRUD ----
  const addRole: AuthContextType['addRole'] = (data) => {
    const name = data.name.trim();
    if (!name) return { ok: false, error: '请输入角色名称' };
    if (roles.some((r) => r.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase())) {
      return { ok: false, error: '角色名称已存在' };
    }
    const newRole: Role = {
      id: `role-${genId()}`,
      name,
      description: data.description.trim(),
      permissions: data.permissions,
      createdAt: nowISO(),
    };
    setRoles((prev) => [...prev, newRole]);
    return { ok: true };
  };

  const updateRole: AuthContextType['updateRole'] = (id, data) => {
    const target = roles.find((r) => r.id === id);
    if (!target) return { ok: false, error: '角色不存在' };
    if (data.name) {
      const name = data.name.trim();
      if (roles.some((r) => r.id !== id && r.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase())) {
        return { ok: false, error: '角色名称已存在' };
      }
    }
    setRoles((prev) =>
      prev.map((r) => {
        if (r.id !== id) return r;
        return {
          ...r,
          name: data.name !== undefined ? data.name.trim() : r.name,
          description: data.description !== undefined ? data.description.trim() : r.description,
          permissions: data.permissions !== undefined ? data.permissions : r.permissions,
        };
      }),
    );
    return { ok: true };
  };

  const deleteRole: AuthContextType['deleteRole'] = (id) => {
    const target = roles.find((r) => r.id === id);
    if (!target) return { ok: false, error: '角色不存在' };
    if (target.isSystem || target.id === 'role-admin') {
      return { ok: false, error: '系统预置角色不可删除' };
    }
    const hasAssignedUsers = users.some((u) => u.roleId === id);
    if (hasAssignedUsers) {
      return { ok: false, error: '该角色下已有用户，请先转移或重新分配用户角色后再删除' };
    }
    setRoles((prev) => prev.filter((r) => r.id !== id));
    return { ok: true };
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        currentUser,
        currentRole,
        username: currentUser?.username ?? null,
        isAuthenticated: !!currentUser,
        loading,
        hasUsers: users.length > 0,
        isAdmin,
        hasPermission,
        login,
        register,
        logout,
        users,
        addUser,
        updateUser,
        deleteUser,
        resetUserPassword,
        toggleUserStatus,
        roles,
        addRole,
        updateRole,
        deleteRole,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth() {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAuth must be used within AuthProvider');
  return ctx;
}
```
New String: ```
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { readJSON, writeJSON, genId, nowISO } from '@/lib/storage';
import { userApi, roleApi, hashPassword } from '@/lib/api';
import {
  type User,
  type Role,
  type PermissionKey,
} from '@/types';

// 仅用于保存当前登录会话（会话标识，非业务数据）
const SESSION_KEY = 'session_user_id';

interface AuthContextType {
  currentUser: User | null;
  currentRole: Role | null;
  username: string | null;
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  loading: boolean;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  hasPermission: (perm: PermissionKey) => boolean;
  login: (username: string, password: string) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }>;
  logout: () => void;
  users: User[];
  addUser: (user: { username: string; realName: string; password: string; roleId: string }) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }>;
  updateUser: (id: string, user: Partial<Omit<User, 'id' | 'createdAt'>>) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }>;
  deleteUser: (id: string) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }>;
  resetUserPassword: (id: string, newPassword?: string) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string; password?: string }>;
  toggleUserStatus: (id: string) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }>;
  roles: Role[];
  addRole: (role: { name: string; description: string; permissions: PermissionKey[] }) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }>;
  updateRole: (id: string, role: { name?: string; description?: string; permissions?: PermissionKey[] }) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }>;
  deleteRole: (id: string) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [currentUserId, setCurrentUserId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [users, setUsers] = useState<User[]>([]);
  const [roles, setRoles] = useState<Role[]>([]);

  // 初始化：从数据库加载用户与角色，并恢复会话
  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true;
    (async () => {
      try {
        const [u, r] = await Promise.all([userApi.list(), roleApi.list()]);
        if (!mounted) return;
        setUsers(u);
        setRoles(r);
        const session = readJSON<string | null>(SESSION_KEY, null);
        if (session && u.some((x) => x.id === session)) {
          setCurrentUserId(session);
        } else {
          writeJSON(SESSION_KEY, null);
        }
      } catch {
        /* 加载失败时保持空列表，登录页可重试 */
      } finally {
        if (mounted) setLoading(false);
      }
    })();
    return () => {
      mounted = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const currentUser = useMemo(() => {
    if (!currentUserId) return null;
    return users.find((u) => u.id === currentUserId) ?? null;
  }, [currentUserId, users]);

  const currentRole = useMemo(() => {
    if (!currentUser) return null;
    return roles.find((r) => r.id === currentUser.roleId) ?? null;
  }, [currentUser, roles]);

  const isAdmin = useMemo(() => {
    if (!currentUser) return false;
    if (currentUser.username === 'admin') return true;
    return currentRole?.id === 'role-admin' || false;
  }, [currentUser, currentRole]);

  const hasPermission = useCallback(
    (perm: PermissionKey): boolean => {
      if (!currentUser) return false;
      if (isAdmin) return true;
      if (!currentRole) return false;
      return currentRole.permissions.includes(perm);
    },
    [currentUser, currentRole, isAdmin],
  );

  const login: AuthContextType['login'] = async (u, p) => {
    const name = u.trim();
    let found: User | null;
    try {
      found = await userApi.findByUsername(name);
    } catch {
      return { ok: false, error: '网络异常，无法连接数据库' };
    }
    if (!found) return { ok: false, error: '用户名或密码错误' };
    if (found.status === 'disabled') return { ok: false, error: '该账号已被禁用，请联系管理员' };
    const inputHash = await hashPassword(p);
    if (found.password !== inputHash) return { ok: false, error: '用户名或密码错误' };

    const now = nowISO();
    setUsers((prev) => prev.map((item) => (item.id === found!.id ? { ...item, lastLoginAt: now } : item)));
    writeJSON(SESSION_KEY, found.id);
    setCurrentUserId(found.id);
    userApi.updateLastLogin(found.id, now).catch(() => undefined);
    return { ok: true };
  };

  const logout = () => {
    writeJSON(SESSION_KEY, null);
    setCurrentUserId(null);
  };

  // ---- 用户 CRUD ----
  const addUser: AuthContextType['addUser'] = async (data) => {
    const name = data.username.trim();
    if (name.length < 2) return { ok: false, error: '用户名至少 2 个字符' };
    if (data.password.length < 4) return { ok: false, error: '密码至少 4 个字符' };
    if (users.some((x) => x.username.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase())) {
      return { ok: false, error: '用户名已存在' };
    }
    const passwordHash = await hashPassword(data.password);
    const newUser: User = {
      id: genId(),
      username: name,
      realName: data.realName.trim() || name,
      password: passwordHash,
      roleId: data.roleId || 'role-operator',
      status: 'active',
      createdAt: nowISO(),
    };
    try {
      await userApi.insert({
        id: newUser.id,
        username: newUser.username,
        realName: newUser.realName,
        password: passwordHash,
        roleId: newUser.roleId,
        status: 'active',
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return { ok: false, error: '保存失败：' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : '未知错误') };
    }
    setUsers((prev) => [...prev, newUser]);
    return { ok: true };
  };

  const updateUser: AuthContextType['updateUser'] = async (id, data) => {
    if (data.username) {
      const name = data.username.trim();
      if (users.some((x) => x.id !== id && x.username.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase())) {
        return { ok: false, error: '该用户名已被占用' };
      }
    }
    const patch: Partial<User> = {};
    if (data.username !== undefined) patch.username = data.username.trim();
    if (data.realName !== undefined) patch.realName = data.realName.trim();
    if (data.roleId !== undefined) patch.roleId = data.roleId;
    try {
      await userApi.update(id, patch);
    } catch (e) {
      return { ok: false, error: '保存失败：' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : '未知错误') };
    }
    setUsers((prev) => prev.map((u) => (u.id === id ? { ...u, ...patch } : u)));
    return { ok: true };
  };

  const deleteUser: AuthContextType['deleteUser'] = async (id) => {
    const target = users.find((u) => u.id === id);
    if (!target) return { ok: false, error: '用户不存在' };
    if (target.id === currentUserId) return { ok: false, error: '无法删除当前正在登录的账号' };
    if (target.username === 'admin') return { ok: false, error: '系统初始管理员账号不可删除' };
    try {
      await userApi.delete(id);
    } catch (e) {
      return { ok: false, error: '删除失败：' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : '未知错误') };
    }
    setUsers((prev) => prev.filter((u) => u.id !== id));
    return { ok: true };
  };

  const resetUserPassword: AuthContextType['resetUserPassword'] = async (id, newPassword) => {
    const pwd = newPassword || '123456';
    const passwordHash = await hashPassword(pwd);
    try {
      await userApi.update(id, { password: passwordHash });
    } catch (e) {
      return { ok: false, error: '重置失败：' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : '未知错误') };
    }
    setUsers((prev) => prev.map((u) => (u.id === id ? { ...u, password: passwordHash } : u)));
    return { ok: true, password: pwd };
  };

  const toggleUserStatus: AuthContextType['toggleUserStatus'] = async (id) => {
    const target = users.find((u) => u.id === id);
    if (!target) return { ok: false, error: '用户不存在' };
    if (target.id === currentUserId) return { ok: false, error: '无法禁用当前正在登录的账号' };
    if (target.username === 'admin') return { ok: false, error: '系统管理员账号不可禁用' };
    const nextStatus = target.status === 'active' ? 'disabled' : 'active';
    try {
      await userApi.update(id, { status: nextStatus });
    } catch (e) {
      return { ok: false, error: '操作失败：' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : '未知错误') };
    }
    setUsers((prev) => prev.map((u) => (u.id === id ? { ...u, status: nextStatus } : u)));
    return { ok: true };
  };

  // ---- 角色 CRUD ----
  const addRole: AuthContextType['addRole'] = async (data) => {
    const name = data.name.trim();
    if (!name) return { ok: false, error: '请输入角色名称' };
    if (roles.some((r) => r.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase())) {
      return { ok: false, error: '角色名称已存在' };
    }
    const newRole: Role = {
      id: `role-${genId()}`,
      name,
      description: data.description.trim(),
      permissions: data.permissions,
      createdAt: nowISO(),
      isSystem: false,
    };
    try {
      await roleApi.insert({
        id: newRole.id,
        name: newRole.name,
        description: newRole.description,
        permissions: newRole.permissions,
        isSystem: false,
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return { ok: false, error: '保存失败：' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : '未知错误') };
    }
    setRoles((prev) => [...prev, newRole]);
    return { ok: true };
  };

  const updateRole: AuthContextType['updateRole'] = async (id, data) => {
    const target = roles.find((r) => r.id === id);
    if (!target) return { ok: false, error: '角色不存在' };
    if (data.name) {
      const name = data.name.trim();
      if (roles.some((r) => r.id !== id && r.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase())) {
        return { ok: false, error: '角色名称已存在' };
      }
    }
    const patch: Partial<Role> = {};
    if (data.name !== undefined) patch.name = data.name.trim();
    if (data.description !== undefined) patch.description = data.description.trim();
    if (data.permissions !== undefined) patch.permissions = data.permissions;
    try {
      await roleApi.update(id, patch);
    } catch (e) {
      return { ok: false, error: '保存失败：' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : '未知错误') };
    }
    setRoles((prev) => prev.map((r) => (r.id === id ? { ...r, ...patch } : r)));
    return { ok: true };
  };

  const deleteRole: AuthContextType['deleteRole'] = async (id) => {
    const target = roles.find((r) => r.id === id);
    if (!target) return { ok: false, error: '角色不存在' };
    if (target.isSystem || target.id === 'role-admin') {
      return { ok: false, error: '系统预置角色不可删除' };
    }
    const hasAssignedUsers = users.some((u) => u.roleId === id);
    if (hasAssignedUsers) {
      return { ok: false, error: '该角色下已有用户，请先转移或重新分配用户角色后再删除' };
    }
    try {
      await roleApi.delete(id);
    } catch (e) {
      return { ok: false, error: '删除失败：' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : '未知错误') };
    }
    setRoles((prev) => prev.filter((r) => r.id !== id));
    return { ok: true };
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        currentUser,
        currentRole,
        username: currentUser?.username ?? null,
        isAuthenticated: !!currentUser,
        loading,
        isAdmin,
        hasPermission,
        login,
        logout,
        users,
        addUser,
        updateUser,
        deleteUser,
        resetUserPassword,
        toggleUserStatus,
        roles,
        addRole,
        updateRole,
        deleteRole,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth() {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAuth must be used within AuthProvider');
  return ctx;
}

    44 export default function BackupPage() {
    45   const { data, createBackup, restoreBackup, deleteBackup, restoreFromFile } = useData();
    46   const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    47   const [restoreTarget, setRestoreTarget] = useState<{ id: string; filename: string } | null>(null);
    48 
    49   const handleBackup = () => {
    50     const rec = createBackup();
    51     downloadText(rec.filename, rec.data);
    52     toast.success(`已创建备份：${rec.filename}`);
    53   };
    54 
    55   const confirmRestore = () => {
    56     if (!restoreTarget) return;
    57     const ok = restoreBackup(restoreTarget.id);
    58     if (ok) toast.success('数据已从备份恢复');
    59     setRestoreTarget(null);
    60   };
    61 
    62   const handleRestoreFile = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    63     const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    64     if (!file) return;
    65     if (!file.name.endsWith('.json')) {
    66       toast.error('仅支持 JSON 备份文件');
    67       e.target.value = '';
    68       return;
    69     }
    70     const text = await file.text();
    71     const res = restoreFromFile(text);
    72     if (res.ok) toast.success('数据已从备份文件恢复');
    73     else toast.error(res.reason ?? '恢复失败');
    74     e.target.value = '';
    75   };
    76

Old String: ```
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { readJSON, writeJSON, genId, nowISO } from '@/lib/storage';
import { parseCIDR, utilizationPercent, findFirstFreeIP, isValidIPv4 } from '@/lib/ip-utils';
import type {
  AppData,
  IPRecord,
  Subnet,
  Vlan,
  AddressPool,
  Device,
  BackupRecord,
  IPStatus,
} from '@/types';

const DATA_KEY = 'app_data';

const emptyData: AppData = {
  ips: [],
  subnets: [],
  vlans: [],
  pools: [],
  devices: [],
  backups: [],
};

interface ConflictInfo {
  conflict: boolean;
  existing?: IPRecord;
}

interface DataContextType {
  data: AppData;
  // IP 台账
  addIP: (rec: Omit<IPRecord, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'>) => ConflictInfo;
  updateIP: (id: string, rec: Partial<IPRecord>) => ConflictInfo;
  deleteIP: (id: string) => void;
  deleteIPs: (ids: string[]) => void;
  forceAddIP: (rec: Omit<IPRecord, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'>, status: IPStatus) => void;
  // 路由器/交换机地址表导入（支持覆盖/跳过/合并冲突处理）
  importIPRecord: (
    rec: Omit<IPRecord, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'>,
    strategy: 'overwrite' | 'skip' | 'merge',
  ) => { added: number; overwritten: number; merged: number; skipped: number };
  // 子网
  addSubnet: (rec: Omit<Subnet, 'id' | 'createdAt'>) => void;
  updateSubnet: (id: string, rec: Partial<Subnet>) => void;
  deleteSubnet: (id: string) => { ok: boolean; reason?: string };
  // VLAN
  addVlan: (rec: Omit<Vlan, 'id' | 'createdAt'>) => void;
  updateVlan: (id: string, rec: Partial<Vlan>) => void;
  deleteVlan: (id: string) => { ok: boolean; reason?: string };
  // 地址池
  addPool: (rec: Omit<AddressPool, 'id' | 'createdAt'>) => void;
  updatePool: (id: string, rec: Partial<AddressPool>) => void;
  deletePool: (id: string) => void;
  allocateFromPool: (poolId: string) => { ok: boolean; ip?: string; reason?: string };
  // 设备
  addDevice: (rec: Omit<Device, 'id' | 'createdAt'>) => void;
  updateDevice: (id: string, rec: Partial<Device>) => void;
  deleteDevice: (id: string) => void;
  // 备份
  createBackup: () => BackupRecord;
  restoreBackup: (backupId: string) => boolean;
  restoreFromFile: (raw: string) => { ok: boolean; reason?: string };
  deleteBackup: (backupId: string) => void;
  // 统计
  subnetStats: (subnetId: string) => { allocated: number; usable: number; percent: number };
}

const DataContext = createContext<DataContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export function DataProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<AppData>(() => readJSON<AppData>(DATA_KEY, emptyData));

  useEffect(() => {
    writeJSON(DATA_KEY, data);
  }, [data]);

  // ---- IP 台账 ----
  const checkConflict = useCallback(
    (ip: string, excludeId?: string): ConflictInfo => {
      const existing = data.ips.find((r) => r.ip === ip && r.id !== excludeId);
      if (existing) return { conflict: true, existing };
      return { conflict: false };
    },
    [data.ips],
  );

  const addIP: DataContextType['addIP'] = useCallback(
    (rec) => {
      const info = checkConflict(rec.ip);
      if (info.conflict) return info;
      const now = nowISO();
      const record: IPRecord = { ...rec, id: genId(), createdAt: now, updatedAt: now };
      setData((d) => ({ ...d, ips: [record, ...d.ips] }));
      return { conflict: false };
    },
    [checkConflict],
  );

  const forceAddIP: DataContextType['forceAddIP'] = useCallback((rec, status) => {
    const now = nowISO();
    const record: IPRecord = { ...rec, status, id: genId(), createdAt: now, updatedAt: now };
    setData((d) => ({
      ...d,
      ips: [record, ...d.ips.map((r) => (r.ip === rec.ip ? { ...r, status: 'conflict' as IPStatus } : r))],
    }));
  }, []);

  const importIPRecord: DataContextType['importIPRecord'] = useCallback(
    (rec, strategy) => {
      const existing = data.ips.find((r) => r.ip === rec.ip);
      if (!existing) {
        const now = nowISO();
        const record: IPRecord = { ...rec, id: genId(), createdAt: now, updatedAt: now };
        setData((d) => ({ ...d, ips: [record, ...d.ips] }));
        return { added: 1, overwritten: 0, merged: 0, skipped: 0 };
      }
      if (strategy === 'skip') {
        return { added: 0, overwritten: 0, merged: 0, skipped: 1 };
      }
      if (strategy === 'overwrite') {
        setData((d) => ({
          ...d,
          ips: d.ips.map((r) => (r.id === existing.id ? { ...r, ...rec, updatedAt: nowISO() } : r)),
        }));
        return { added: 0, overwritten: 1, merged: 0, skipped: 0 };
      }
      // merge：仅补充原记录的空白字段
      const mergedRec: IPRecord = {
        ...existing,
        hostname: existing.hostname || rec.hostname,
        mac: existing.mac || rec.mac,
        purpose: existing.purpose || rec.purpose,
        note: existing.note || rec.note,
        subnetId: existing.subnetId ?? rec.subnetId,
        updatedAt: nowISO(),
      };
      setData((d) => ({
        ...d,
        ips: d.ips.map((r) => (r.id === existing.id ? mergedRec : r)),
      }));
      return { added: 0, overwritten: 0, merged: 1, skipped: 0 };
    },
    [data.ips],
  );

  const updateIP: DataContextType['updateIP'] = useCallback(
    (id, rec) => {
      if (rec.ip) {
        const info = checkConflict(rec.ip, id);
        if (info.conflict) return info;
      }
      setData((d) => ({
        ...d,
        ips: d.ips.map((r) => (r.id === id ? { ...r, ...rec, updatedAt: nowISO() } : r)),
      }));
      return { conflict: false };
    },
    [checkConflict],
  );

  const deleteIP: DataContextType['deleteIP'] = useCallback((id) => {
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, ips: d.ips.filter((r) => r.id !== id) }));
  }, []);

  const deleteIPs: DataContextType['deleteIPs'] = useCallback((ids) => {
    const idSet = new Set(ids);
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, ips: d.ips.filter((r) => !idSet.has(r.id)) }));
  }, []);

  // ---- 子网 ----
  const addSubnet: DataContextType['addSubnet'] = useCallback((rec) => {
    const record: Subnet = { ...rec, id: genId(), createdAt: nowISO() };
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, subnets: [record, ...d.subnets] }));
  }, []);

  const updateSubnet: DataContextType['updateSubnet'] = useCallback((id, rec) => {
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, subnets: d.subnets.map((s) => (s.id === id ? { ...s, ...rec } : s)) }));
  }, []);

  const deleteSubnet: DataContextType['deleteSubnet'] = useCallback((id) => {
    const hasIP = data.ips.some((r) => r.subnetId === id);
    if (hasIP) return { ok: false, reason: '该子网下存在 IP 记录，请先删除或迁移后再删除子网' };
    setData((d) => ({
      ...d,
      subnets: d.subnets.filter((s) => s.id !== id),
      vlans: d.vlans.map((v) => ({ ...v, subnetIds: v.subnetIds.filter((sid) => sid !== id) })),
      pools: d.pools.map((p) => (p.subnetId === id ? { ...p, subnetId: null } : p)),
    }));
    return { ok: true };
  }, [data.ips]);

  // ---- VLAN ----
  const addVlan: DataContextType['addVlan'] = useCallback((rec) => {
    const record: Vlan = { ...rec, id: genId(), createdAt: nowISO() };
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, vlans: [record, ...d.vlans] }));
  }, []);

  const updateVlan: DataContextType['updateVlan'] = useCallback((id, rec) => {
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, vlans: d.vlans.map((v) => (v.id === id ? { ...v, ...rec } : v)) }));
  }, []);

  const deleteVlan: DataContextType['deleteVlan'] = useCallback((id) => {
    const vlan = data.vlans.find((v) => v.id === id);
    if (vlan && vlan.subnetIds.length > 0) {
      return { ok: false, reason: '该 VLAN 仍关联子网，请先解除关联后再删除' };
    }
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, vlans: d.vlans.filter((v) => v.id !== id) }));
    return { ok: true };
  }, [data.vlans]);

  // ---- 地址池 ----
  const addPool: DataContextType['addPool'] = useCallback((rec) => {
    const record: AddressPool = { ...rec, id: genId(), createdAt: nowISO() };
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, pools: [record, ...d.pools] }));
  }, []);

  const updatePool: DataContextType['updatePool'] = useCallback((id, rec) => {
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, pools: d.pools.map((p) => (p.id === id ? { ...p, ...rec } : p)) }));
  }, []);

  const deletePool: DataContextType['deletePool'] = useCallback((id) => {
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, pools: d.pools.filter((p) => p.id !== id) }));
  }, []);

  const allocateFromPool: DataContextType['allocateFromPool'] = useCallback(
    (poolId) => {
      const pool = data.pools.find((p) => p.id === poolId);
      if (!pool) return { ok: false, reason: '地址池不存在' };
      if (!isValidIPv4(pool.startIp) || !isValidIPv4(pool.endIp)) {
        return { ok: false, reason: '地址池范围无效（仅支持 IPv4 自动分配）' };
      }
      const used = data.ips.map((r) => r.ip);
      const free = findFirstFreeIP(pool.startIp, pool.endIp, used);
      if (!free) return { ok: false, reason: '地址池内已无可用 IP，地址池已满' };
      const now = nowISO();
      const record: IPRecord = {
        id: genId(),
        ip: free,
        hostname: '',
        mac: '',
        purpose: pool.purpose || '地址池分配',
        subnetId: pool.subnetId,
        status: 'allocated',
        note: `来自地址池「${pool.name}」`,
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      };
      setData((d) => ({ ...d, ips: [record, ...d.ips] }));
      return { ok: true, ip: free };
    },
    [data.pools, data.ips],
  );

  // ---- 设备 ----
  const addDevice: DataContextType['addDevice'] = useCallback((rec) => {
    const record: Device = { ...rec, id: genId(), createdAt: nowISO() };
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, devices: [record, ...d.devices] }));
  }, []);

  const updateDevice: DataContextType['updateDevice'] = useCallback((id, rec) => {
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, devices: d.devices.map((dv) => (dv.id === id ? { ...dv, ...rec } : dv)) }));
  }, []);

  const deleteDevice: DataContextType['deleteDevice'] = useCallback((id) => {
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, devices: d.devices.filter((dv) => dv.id !== id) }));
  }, []);

  // ---- 备份 ----
  const createBackup: DataContextType['createBackup'] = useCallback(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
    const stamp = `${now.getFullYear()}${pad(now.getMonth() + 1)}${pad(now.getDate())}_${pad(now.getHours())}${pad(now.getMinutes())}${pad(now.getSeconds())}`;
    const payload = {
      ips: data.ips,
      subnets: data.subnets,
      vlans: data.vlans,
      pools: data.pools,
      devices: data.devices,
      exportedAt: now.toISOString(),
    };
    const dataStr = JSON.stringify(payload);
    const record: BackupRecord = {
      id: genId(),
      filename: `IPAM_备份_${stamp}.json`,
      timestamp: now.toISOString(),
      size: dataStr.length,
      data: dataStr,
    };
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, backups: [record, ...d.backups] }));
    return record;
  }, [data]);

  const restoreBackup: DataContextType['restoreBackup'] = useCallback((backupId) => {
    const backup = data.backups.find((b) => b.id === backupId);
    if (!backup) return false;
    try {
      const payload = JSON.parse(backup.data);
      setData((d) => ({
        ...d,
        ips: payload.ips ?? [],
        subnets: payload.subnets ?? [],
        vlans: payload.vlans ?? [],
        pools: payload.pools ?? [],
        devices: payload.devices ?? [],
      }));
      return true;
    } catch {
      toast.error('备份文件损坏或格式错误，无法恢复');
      return false;
    }
  }, [data.backups]);

  const restoreFromFile: DataContextType['restoreFromFile'] = useCallback((raw) => {
    try {
      const payload = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (!payload || typeof payload !== 'object') {
        return { ok: false, reason: '备份文件格式无效' };
      }
      setData((d) => ({
        ...d,
        ips: Array.isArray(payload.ips) ? payload.ips : [],
        subnets: Array.isArray(payload.subnets) ? payload.subnets : [],
        vlans: Array.isArray(payload.vlans) ? payload.vlans : [],
        pools: Array.isArray(payload.pools) ? payload.pools : [],
        devices: Array.isArray(payload.devices) ? payload.devices : [],
      }));
      return { ok: true };
    } catch {
      return { ok: false, reason: '备份文件损坏或格式错误，无法恢复' };
    }
  }, []);

  const deleteBackup: DataContextType['deleteBackup'] = useCallback((backupId) => {
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, backups: d.backups.filter((b) => b.id !== backupId) }));
  }, []);

  // ---- 统计 ----
  const subnetStats: DataContextType['subnetStats'] = useCallback(
    (subnetId) => {
      const subnet = data.subnets.find((s) => s.id === subnetId);
      const info = subnet ? parseCIDR(subnet.cidr) : null;
      const usable = info ? info.usable : 0;
      const allocated = data.ips.filter((r) => r.subnetId === subnetId && r.status !== 'unallocated').length;
      return { allocated, usable, percent: utilizationPercent(allocated, usable) };
    },
    [data.subnets, data.ips],
  );

  const value = useMemo<DataContextType>(
    () => ({
      data,
      addIP,
      updateIP,
      deleteIP,
      deleteIPs,
      forceAddIP,
      importIPRecord,
      addSubnet,
      updateSubnet,
      deleteSubnet,
      addVlan,
      updateVlan,
      deleteVlan,
      addPool,
      updatePool,
      deletePool,
      allocateFromPool,
      addDevice,
      updateDevice,
      deleteDevice,
      createBackup,
      restoreBackup,
      restoreFromFile,
      deleteBackup,
      subnetStats,
    }),
    [
      data,
      addIP,
      updateIP,
      deleteIP,
      deleteIPs,
      forceAddIP,
      importIPRecord,
      addSubnet,
      updateSubnet,
      deleteSubnet,
      addVlan,
      updateVlan,
      deleteVlan,
      addPool,
      updatePool,
      deletePool,
      allocateFromPool,
      addDevice,
      updateDevice,
      deleteDevice,
      createBackup,
      restoreBackup,
      restoreFromFile,
      deleteBackup,
      subnetStats,
    ],
  );

  return <DataContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</DataContext.Provider>;
}

export function useData() {
  const ctx = useContext(DataContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useData must be used within DataProvider');
  return ctx;
}
```
New String: ```
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { genId, nowISO } from '@/lib/storage';
import { loadAllData, ipApi, subnetApi, vlanApi, poolApi, deviceApi, backupApi } from '@/lib/api';
import { parseCIDR, utilizationPercent, findFirstFreeIP, isValidIPv4 } from '@/lib/ip-utils';
import type {
  AppData,
  IPRecord,
  Subnet,
  Vlan,
  AddressPool,
  Device,
  BackupRecord,
  IPStatus,
} from '@/types';

const emptyData: AppData = {
  ips: [],
  subnets: [],
  vlans: [],
  pools: [],
  devices: [],
  backups: [],
};

interface ConflictInfo {
  conflict: boolean;
  existing?: IPRecord;
}

interface DataContextType {
  data: AppData;
  loading: boolean;
  reload: () => Promise<void>;
  // IP 台账
  addIP: (rec: Omit<IPRecord, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'>) => ConflictInfo;
  updateIP: (id: string, rec: Partial<IPRecord>) => ConflictInfo;
  deleteIP: (id: string) => void;
  deleteIPs: (ids: string[]) => void;
  forceAddIP: (rec: Omit<IPRecord, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'>, status: IPStatus) => void;
  importIPRecord: (
    rec: Omit<IPRecord, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt'>,
    strategy: 'overwrite' | 'skip' | 'merge',
  ) => { added: number; overwritten: number; merged: number; skipped: number };
  // 子网
  addSubnet: (rec: Omit<Subnet, 'id' | 'createdAt'>) => void;
  updateSubnet: (id: string, rec: Partial<Subnet>) => void;
  deleteSubnet: (id: string) => { ok: boolean; reason?: string };
  // VLAN
  addVlan: (rec: Omit<Vlan, 'id' | 'createdAt'>) => void;
  updateVlan: (id: string, rec: Partial<Vlan>) => void;
  deleteVlan: (id: string) => { ok: boolean; reason?: string };
  // 地址池
  addPool: (rec: Omit<AddressPool, 'id' | 'createdAt'>) => void;
  updatePool: (id: string, rec: Partial<AddressPool>) => void;
  deletePool: (id: string) => void;
  allocateFromPool: (poolId: string) => { ok: boolean; ip?: string; reason?: string };
  // 设备
  addDevice: (rec: Omit<Device, 'id' | 'createdAt'>) => void;
  updateDevice: (id: string, rec: Partial<Device>) => void;
  deleteDevice: (id: string) => void;
  // 备份
  createBackup: () => BackupRecord;
  restoreBackup: (backupId: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  restoreFromFile: (raw: string) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; reason?: string }>;
  deleteBackup: (backupId: string) => void;
  // 统计
  subnetStats: (subnetId: string) => { allocated: number; usable: number; percent: number };
}

const DataContext = createContext<DataContextType | undefined>(undefined);

// 后台持久化：乐观更新后异步写库，失败仅提示
function persist(fn: () => Promise<void>, label: string) {
  fn().catch((e) => toast.error(`${label}失败：${e instanceof Error ? e.message : '请检查网络'}`));
}

export function DataProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<AppData>(emptyData);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const reload = useCallback(async () => {
    const fresh = await loadAllData();
    setData(fresh);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true;
    (async () => {
      try {
        const fresh = await loadAllData();
        if (mounted) setData(fresh);
      } catch {
        /* 加载失败保持空数据 */
      } finally {
        if (mounted) setLoading(false);
      }
    })();
    return () => {
      mounted = false;
    };
  }, []);

  // ---- IP 台账 ----
  const checkConflict = useCallback(
    (ip: string, excludeId?: string): ConflictInfo => {
      const existing = data.ips.find((r) => r.ip === ip && r.id !== excludeId);
      if (existing) return { conflict: true, existing };
      return { conflict: false };
    },
    [data.ips],
  );

  const addIP: DataContextType['addIP'] = useCallback(
    (rec) => {
      const info = checkConflict(rec.ip);
      if (info.conflict) return info;
      const now = nowISO();
      const record: IPRecord = { ...rec, id: genId(), createdAt: now, updatedAt: now };
      setData((d) => ({ ...d, ips: [record, ...d.ips] }));
      persist(() => ipApi.insert({ ...record }), '保存IP');
      return { conflict: false };
    },
    [checkConflict],
  );

  const forceAddIP: DataContextType['forceAddIP'] = useCallback((rec, status) => {
    const now = nowISO();
    const record: IPRecord = { ...rec, status, id: genId(), createdAt: now, updatedAt: now };
    setData((d) => ({
      ...d,
      ips: [record, ...d.ips.map((r) => (r.ip === rec.ip ? { ...r, status: 'conflict' as IPStatus } : r))],
    }));
    persist(() => ipApi.insert({ ...record }), '保存IP');
  }, []);

  const importIPRecord: DataContextType['importIPRecord'] = useCallback(
    (rec, strategy) => {
      const existing = data.ips.find((r) => r.ip === rec.ip);
      if (!existing) {
        const now = nowISO();
        const record: IPRecord = { ...rec, id: genId(), createdAt: now, updatedAt: now };
        setData((d) => ({ ...d, ips: [record, ...d.ips] }));
        persist(() => ipApi.insert({ ...record }), '导入IP');
        return { added: 1, overwritten: 0, merged: 0, skipped: 0 };
      }
      if (strategy === 'skip') {
        return { added: 0, overwritten: 0, merged: 0, skipped: 1 };
      }
      if (strategy === 'overwrite') {
        const now = nowISO();
        setData((d) => ({
          ...d,
          ips: d.ips.map((r) => (r.id === existing.id ? { ...r, ...rec, updatedAt: now } : r)),
        }));
        persist(() => ipApi.update(existing.id, { ...rec, updatedAt: now }), '导入IP');
        return { added: 0, overwritten: 1, merged: 0, skipped: 0 };
      }
      const mergedRec: IPRecord = {
        ...existing,
        hostname: existing.hostname || rec.hostname,
        mac: existing.mac || rec.mac,
        purpose: existing.purpose || rec.purpose,
        note: existing.note || rec.note,
        subnetId: existing.subnetId ?? rec.subnetId,
        updatedAt: nowISO(),
      };
      setData((d) => ({
        ...d,
        ips: d.ips.map((r) => (r.id === existing.id ? mergedRec : r)),
      }));
      persist(() => ipApi.update(existing.id, mergedRec), '导入IP');
      return { added: 0, overwritten: 0, merged: 1, skipped: 0 };
    },
    [data.ips],
  );

  const updateIP: DataContextType['updateIP'] = useCallback(
    (id, rec) => {
      if (rec.ip) {
        const info = checkConflict(rec.ip, id);
        if (info.conflict) return info;
      }
      const now = nowISO();
      setData((d) => ({
        ...d,
        ips: d.ips.map((r) => (r.id === id ? { ...r, ...rec, updatedAt: now } : r)),
      }));
      persist(() => ipApi.update(id, { ...rec, updatedAt: now }), '更新IP');
      return { conflict: false };
    },
    [checkConflict],
  );

  const deleteIP: DataContextType['deleteIP'] = useCallback((id) => {
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, ips: d.ips.filter((r) => r.id !== id) }));
    persist(() => ipApi.delete(id), '删除IP');
  }, []);

  const deleteIPs: DataContextType['deleteIPs'] = useCallback((ids) => {
    const idSet = new Set(ids);
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, ips: d.ips.filter((r) => !idSet.has(r.id)) }));
    persist(() => ipApi.deleteMany(ids), '批量删除IP');
  }, []);

  // ---- 子网 ----
  const addSubnet: DataContextType['addSubnet'] = useCallback((rec) => {
    const record: Subnet = { ...rec, id: genId(), createdAt: nowISO() };
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, subnets: [record, ...d.subnets] }));
    persist(() => subnetApi.insert({ ...record }), '新增子网');
  }, []);

  const updateSubnet: DataContextType['updateSubnet'] = useCallback((id, rec) => {
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, subnets: d.subnets.map((s) => (s.id === id ? { ...s, ...rec } : s)) }));
    persist(() => subnetApi.update(id, rec), '更新子网');
  }, []);

  const deleteSubnet: DataContextType['deleteSubnet'] = useCallback(
    (id) => {
      const hasIP = data.ips.some((r) => r.subnetId === id);
      if (hasIP) return { ok: false, reason: '该子网下存在 IP 记录，请先删除或迁移后再删除子网' };
      setData((d) => ({
        ...d,
        subnets: d.subnets.filter((s) => s.id !== id),
        vlans: d.vlans.map((v) => ({ ...v, subnetIds: v.subnetIds.filter((sid) => sid !== id) })),
        pools: d.pools.map((p) => (p.subnetId === id ? { ...p, subnetId: null } : p)),
      }));
      persist(() => subnetApi.delete(id), '删除子网');
      return { ok: true };
    },
    [data.ips],
  );

  // ---- VLAN ----
  const addVlan: DataContextType['addVlan'] = useCallback((rec) => {
    const record: Vlan = { ...rec, id: genId(), createdAt: nowISO() };
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, vlans: [record, ...d.vlans] }));
    persist(() => vlanApi.insert({ ...record }), '新增VLAN');
  }, []);

  const updateVlan: DataContextType['updateVlan'] = useCallback((id, rec) => {
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, vlans: d.vlans.map((v) => (v.id === id ? { ...v, ...rec } : v)) }));
    persist(() => vlanApi.update(id, rec), '更新VLAN');
  }, []);

  const deleteVlan: DataContextType['deleteVlan'] = useCallback(
    (id) => {
      const vlan = data.vlans.find((v) => v.id === id);
      if (vlan && vlan.subnetIds.length > 0) {
        return { ok: false, reason: '该 VLAN 仍关联子网，请先解除关联后再删除' };
      }
      setData((d) => ({ ...d, vlans: d.vlans.filter((v) => v.id !== id) }));
      persist(() => vlanApi.delete(id), '删除VLAN');
      return { ok: true };
    },
    [data.vlans],
  );

  // ---- 地址池 ----
  const addPool: DataContextType['addPool'] = useCallback((rec) => {
    const record: AddressPool = { ...rec, id: genId(), createdAt: nowISO() };
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, pools: [record, ...d.pools] }));
    persist(() => poolApi.insert({ ...record }), '新增地址池');
  }, []);

  const updatePool: DataContextType['updatePool'] = useCallback((id, rec) => {
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, pools: d.pools.map((p) => (p.id === id ? { ...p, ...rec } : p)) }));
    persist(() => poolApi.update(id, rec), '更新地址池');
  }, []);

  const deletePool: DataContextType['deletePool'] = useCallback((id) => {
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, pools: d.pools.filter((p) => p.id !== id) }));
    persist(() => poolApi.delete(id), '删除地址池');
  }, []);

  const allocateFromPool: DataContextType['allocateFromPool'] = useCallback(
    (poolId) => {
      const pool = data.pools.find((p) => p.id === poolId);
      if (!pool) return { ok: false, reason: '地址池不存在' };
      if (!isValidIPv4(pool.startIp) || !isValidIPv4(pool.endIp)) {
        return { ok: false, reason: '地址池范围无效（仅支持 IPv4 自动分配）' };
      }
      const used = data.ips.map((r) => r.ip);
      const free = findFirstFreeIP(pool.startIp, pool.endIp, used);
      if (!free) return { ok: false, reason: '地址池内已无可用 IP，地址池已满' };
      const now = nowISO();
      const record: IPRecord = {
        id: genId(),
        ip: free,
        hostname: '',
        mac: '',
        purpose: pool.purpose || '地址池分配',
        subnetId: pool.subnetId,
        status: 'allocated',
        note: `来自地址池「${pool.name}」`,
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      };
      setData((d) => ({ ...d, ips: [record, ...d.ips] }));
      persist(() => ipApi.insert({ ...record }), '分配IP');
      return { ok: true, ip: free };
    },
    [data.pools, data.ips],
  );

  // ---- 设备 ----
  const addDevice: DataContextType['addDevice'] = useCallback((rec) => {
    const record: Device = { ...rec, id: genId(), createdAt: nowISO() };
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, devices: [record, ...d.devices] }));
    persist(() => deviceApi.insert({ ...record }), '新增设备');
  }, []);

  const updateDevice: DataContextType['updateDevice'] = useCallback((id, rec) => {
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, devices: d.devices.map((dv) => (dv.id === id ? { ...dv, ...rec } : dv)) }));
    persist(() => deviceApi.update(id, rec), '更新设备');
  }, []);

  const deleteDevice: DataContextType['deleteDevice'] = useCallback((id) => {
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, devices: d.devices.filter((dv) => dv.id !== id) }));
    persist(() => deviceApi.delete(id), '删除设备');
  }, []);

  // ---- 备份 ----
  const createBackup: DataContextType['createBackup'] = useCallback(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
    const stamp = `${now.getFullYear()}${pad(now.getMonth() + 1)}${pad(now.getDate())}_${pad(now.getHours())}${pad(now.getMinutes())}${pad(now.getSeconds())}`;
    const payload = {
      ips: data.ips,
      subnets: data.subnets,
      vlans: data.vlans,
      pools: data.pools,
      devices: data.devices,
      exportedAt: now.toISOString(),
    };
    const dataStr = JSON.stringify(payload);
    const record: BackupRecord = {
      id: genId(),
      filename: `IPAM_备份_${stamp}.json`,
      timestamp: now.toISOString(),
      size: dataStr.length,
      data: dataStr,
    };
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, backups: [record, ...d.backups] }));
    persist(() => backupApi.insert({ id: record.id, filename: record.filename, timestamp: record.timestamp, size: record.size, data: record.data }), '创建备份');
    return record;
  }, [data]);

  const restoreBackup: DataContextType['restoreBackup'] = useCallback(
    async (backupId) => {
      const backup = data.backups.find((b) => b.id === backupId);
      if (!backup) return false;
      try {
        const payload = JSON.parse(backup.data);
        await backupApi.restore({
          ips: Array.isArray(payload.ips) ? payload.ips : [],
          subnets: Array.isArray(payload.subnets) ? payload.subnets : [],
          vlans: Array.isArray(payload.vlans) ? payload.vlans : [],
          pools: Array.isArray(payload.pools) ? payload.pools : [],
          devices: Array.isArray(payload.devices) ? payload.devices : [],
        });
        await reload();
        return true;
      } catch {
        toast.error('备份文件损坏或格式错误，无法恢复');
        return false;
      }
    },
    [data.backups, reload],
  );

  const restoreFromFile: DataContextType['restoreFromFile'] = useCallback(
    async (raw) => {
      try {
        const payload = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (!payload || typeof payload !== 'object') {
          return { ok: false, reason: '备份文件格式无效' };
        }
        await backupApi.restore({
          ips: Array.isArray(payload.ips) ? payload.ips : [],
          subnets: Array.isArray(payload.subnets) ? payload.subnets : [],
          vlans: Array.isArray(payload.vlans) ? payload.vlans : [],
          pools: Array.isArray(payload.pools) ? payload.pools : [],
          devices: Array.isArray(payload.devices) ? payload.devices : [],
        });
        await reload();
        return { ok: true };
      } catch {
        return { ok: false, reason: '备份文件损坏或格式错误，无法恢复' };
      }
    },
    [reload],
  );

  const deleteBackup: DataContextType['deleteBackup'] = useCallback((backupId) => {
    setData((d) => ({ ...d, backups: d.backups.filter((b) => b.id !== backupId) }));
    persist(() => backupApi.delete(backupId), '删除备份');
  }, []);

  // ---- 统计 ----
  const subnetStats: DataContextType['subnetStats'] = useCallback(
    (subnetId) => {
      const subnet = data.subnets.find((s) => s.id === subnetId);
      const info = subnet ? parseCIDR(subnet.cidr) : null;
      const usable = info ? info.usable : 0;
      const allocated = data.ips.filter((r) => r.subnetId === subnetId && r.status !== 'unallocated').length;
      return { allocated, usable, percent: utilizationPercent(allocated, usable) };
    },
    [data.subnets, data.ips],
  );

  const value = useMemo<DataContextType>(
    () => ({
      data,
      loading,
      reload,
      addIP,
      updateIP,
      deleteIP,
      deleteIPs,
      forceAddIP,
      importIPRecord,
      addSubnet,
      updateSubnet,
      deleteSubnet,
      addVlan,
      updateVlan,
      deleteVlan,
      addPool,
      updatePool,
      deletePool,
      allocateFromPool,
      addDevice,
      updateDevice,
      deleteDevice,
      createBackup,
      restoreBackup,
      restoreFromFile,
      deleteBackup,
      subnetStats,
    }),
    [
      data,
      loading,
      reload,
      addIP,
      updateIP,
      deleteIP,
      deleteIPs,
      forceAddIP,
      importIPRecord,
      addSubnet,
      updateSubnet,
      deleteSubnet,
      addVlan,
      updateVlan,
      deleteVlan,
      addPool,
      updatePool,
      deletePool,
      allocateFromPool,
      addDevice,
      updateDevice,
      deleteDevice,
      createBackup,
      restoreBackup,
      restoreFromFile,
      deleteBackup,
      subnetStats,
    ],
  );

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-background">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-muted-foreground">
          <span className="h-3 w-3 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-primary" />
          <span className="text-sm">正在加载数据…</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return <DataContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</DataContext.Provider>;
}

export function useData() {
  const ctx = useContext(DataContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useData must be used within DataProvider');
  return ctx;
}

New String: ```
import { useState } from 'react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { useAuth } from '@/contexts/AuthContext';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';

export default function LoginPage() {
  const { login } = useAuth();
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!username.trim() || !password) {
      toast.error('请输入用户名和密码');
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    const res = await login(username, password);
    setLoading(false);
    if (!res.ok) {
      toast.error(res.error ?? '登录失败');
      return;
    }
    toast.success('登录成功');
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen w-full items-center justify-center bg-background p-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md">
        <div className="mb-8 flex flex-col items-center text-center">
          <img src="./logo.png" alt="Logo" className="mb-4 h-16 w-16 rounded-2xl object-contain shadow-md" />
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">IP台账管理</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground text-pretty">
            面向飞牛 OS 的 IP 地址管理系统 · 数据云端持久保存
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-6">
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="username">用户名</Label>
              <Input
                id="username"
                value={username}
                onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="请输入用户名"
                autoComplete="username"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="password">密码</Label>
              <Input
                id="password"
                type="password"
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="请输入密码"
                autoComplete="current-password"
              />
            </div>
            <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={loading}>
              {loading ? '登录中…' : '登录'}
            </Button>
          </form>
        </div>

        <p className="mt-6 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          默认管理员账号 admin / admin，新账号请由管理员在「用户管理」中创建
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

const handleSave = () => {
    if (!form.username.trim()) {
      toast.error('请输入用户名');
      return;
    }
    if (!editingUser && !form.password) {
      toast.error('新建用户必须设置初始密码');
      return;
    }
    if (!editingUser && form.password.length < 4) {
      toast.error('密码长度至少 4 位');
      return;
    }

    if (editingUser) {
      const res = updateUser(editingUser.id, {
        username: form.username.trim(),
        realName: form.realName.trim(),
        roleId: form.roleId,
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        toast.error(res.error);
        return;
      }
      toast.success('用户信息已更新');
      setDialogOpen(false);
    } else {
      const res = addUser({
        username: form.username.trim(),
        realName: form.realName.trim(),
        password: form.password,
        roleId: form.roleId,
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        toast.error(res.error);
        return;
      }
      toast.success('用户已成功创建');
      setDialogOpen(false);
    }
  };

  const confirmDelete = () => {
    if (!deleteTarget) return;
    const res = deleteUser(deleteTarget.id);
    if (!res.ok) {
      toast.error(res.error);
    } else {
      toast.success('用户已删除');
    }
    setDeleteTarget(null);
  };

  const handleResetPassword = () => {
    if (!resetTarget) return;
    const pwd = newPassword.trim() || '123456';
    const res = resetUserPassword(resetTarget.id, pwd);
    if (!res.ok) {
      toast.error(res.error);
    } else {
      toast.success(`用户 ${resetTarget.username} 的密码已重置为：${pwd}`);
      setResetResult(pwd);
    }
    setResetTarget(null);
    setNewPassword('');
  };

  const handleToggleStatus = (user: User) => {
    const res = toggleUserStatus(user.id);
    if (!res.ok) {
      toast.error(res.error);

      toast.success(
        user.status === 'active'
          ? `已禁用用户 ${user.username}`

const handleSave = async () => {
    if (!form.username.trim()) {
      toast.error('请输入用户名');
      return;
    }
    if (!editingUser && !form.password) {
      toast.error('新建用户必须设置初始密码');
      return;
    }
    if (!editingUser && form.password.length < 4) {
      toast.error('密码长度至少 4 位');
      return;
    }

    if (editingUser) {
      const res = await updateUser(editingUser.id, {
        username: form.username.trim(),
        realName: form.realName.trim(),
        roleId: form.roleId,
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        toast.error(res.error);
        return;
      }
      toast.success('用户信息已更新');
      setDialogOpen(false);
    } else {
      const res = await addUser({
        username: form.username.trim(),
        realName: form.realName.trim(),
        password: form.password,
        roleId: form.roleId,
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        toast.error(res.error);
        return;
      }
      toast.success('用户已成功创建');
      setDialogOpen(false);
    }
  };

  const confirmDelete = async () => {
    if (!deleteTarget) return;

New String: ```
// ============ camelCase -> snake_case 反向映射（用于备份恢复批量写入） ============
function toSnakeIP(r: IPRecord) {
  return { id: r.id, ip: r.ip, hostname: r.hostname, mac: r.mac, purpose: r.purpose, subnet_id: r.subnetId, status: r.status, note: r.note, created_at: r.createdAt, updated_at: r.updatedAt };
}
function toSnakeSubnet(r: Subnet) {
  return { id: r.id, cidr: r.cidr, name: r.name, gateway: r.gateway, version: r.version, note: r.note, created_at: r.createdAt };
}
function toSnakeVlan(r: Vlan) {
  return { id: r.id, vlan_id: r.vlanId, name: r.name, gateway: r.gateway, subnet_ids: r.subnetIds, note: r.note, created_at: r.createdAt };
}
function toSnakePool(r: AddressPool) {
  return { id: r.id, name: r.name, start_ip: r.startIp, end_ip: r.endIp, subnet_id: r.subnetId, purpose: r.purpose, note: r.note, created_at: r.createdAt };
}
function toSnakeDevice(r: Device) {
  return { id: r.id, name: r.name, type: r.type, location: r.location, ip: r.ip, mac: r.mac, note: r.note, created_at: r.createdAt };
}

// ============ 业务数据加载 ============
```

// 恢复：先清空再批量写入
  restore: async (payload: {
    ips: IPRecord[];
    subnets: Subnet[];
    vlans: Vlan[];
    pools: AddressPool[];
    devices: Device[];
  }) => {
    const tables = ['ips', 'subnets', 'vlans', 'pools', 'devices'] as const;
    for (const t of tables) {
      const { error: delErr } = await supabase.from(t).delete().neq('id', '__none__');
      if (delErr) throw delErr;
      const rows = payload[t];
      if (rows.length > 0) {
        const { error: insErr } = await supabase.from(t).insert(rows);
        if (insErr) throw insErr;
      }
    }
  },
```
New String: ```
// 恢复：先清空再批量写入（需将 camelCase 转为 snake_case）
  restore: async (payload: {
    ips: IPRecord[];
    subnets: Subnet[];
    vlans: Vlan[];
    pools: AddressPool[];
    devices: Device[];
  }) => {
    const ops: Array<[string, Record<string, unknown>[]]> = [
      ['ips', payload.ips.map(toSnakeIP)],
      ['subnets', payload.subnets.map(toSnakeSubnet)],
      ['vlans', payload.vlans.map(toSnakeVlan)],
      ['pools', payload.pools.map(toSnakePool)],
      ['devices', payload.devices.map(toSnakeDevice)],
    ];
    for (const [t, rows] of ops) {
      const { error: delErr } = await supabase.from(t).delete().neq('id', '__none__');
      if (delErr) throw delErr;
      if (rows.length > 0) {
        const { error: insErr } = await supabase.from(t).insert(rows as never[]);
        if (insErr) throw insErr;
      }
    }
  },
```

Here's the result of running `cat -n` on the file: /workspace/app-e48he9p4w001/scripts/package-fpk.mjs:
     1 /**
     2  * 打包飞牛 fnOS 应用中心安装包（.fpk）—— 对齐官方 fnpack 规范
     3  *
     4  * 用法：node scripts/package-fpk.mjs [version]
     5  * 产物：packaging/ipam-<version>.fpk
     6  *
     7  * .fpk 结构（官方 fnpack 项目布局）：
     8  *   manifest / ICON.PNG / ICON_256.PNG / health.json
     9  *   cmd/  config/  wizard/  app.tgz（www 静态站点 + docker 编排 + ui 入口资源）
    10  */
    11 import { execSync } from 'child_process';
    12 import fs from 'fs';
    13 import path from 'path';
    14 import crypto from 'crypto';
    15 import os from 'os';
    16 
    17 const ROOT = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, '..');
    18 const DIST = path.join(ROOT, 'dist');
    19 const PKG_DIR = path.join(ROOT, 'packaging', 'fnos-app', 'ipam');
    20 const VERSION = process.argv[2] || '1.0.1';
    21 const STAGE = path.join(os.tmpdir(), `ipam-fpk-${Date.now()}`);
    22 const OUT_DIR = path.join(ROOT, 'packaging');
    23 const FPK = path.join(OUT_DIR, `ipam-${VERSION}.fpk`);
    24 
    25 function sh(cmd, cwd) {
    26   execSync(cmd, { cwd, stdio: 'inherit', shell: true });
    27 }
    28 function rm(p) {
    29   fs.rmSync(p, { recursive: true, force: true });
    30 }
    31 
    32 // 1. 构建生产版本 dist
    33 if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(DIST, 'index.html'))) {
    34   console.log('>> 构建 dist ...');
    35   sh('npx vite build --minify false', ROOT);
    36 } else {
    37   console.log('>> 使用已有 dist 构建产物');
    38 }
    39 
    40 rm(STAGE);
    41 fs.mkdirSync(path.join(STAGE, 'app'), { recursive: true });
    42 
    43 // 2. 组装 app/（www 静态站点 + docker 编排 + ui 入口资源）→ app.tgz
    44 console.log('>> 组装 app.tgz ...');
    45 fs.cpSync(DIST, path.join(STAGE, 'app', 'www'), { recursive: true });
    46 fs.cpSync(path.join(PKG_DIR, 'app', 'docker'), path.join(STAGE, 'app', 'docker'), { recursive: true });
    47 fs.cpSync(path.join(PKG_DIR, 'app', 'ui'), path.join(STAGE, 'app', 'ui'), { recursive: true });
    48 sh('tar -czf app.tgz -C app .', STAGE);
    49 rm(path.join(STAGE, 'app'));
    50 
    51 // 3. manifest 写入 app.tgz 的 md5 校验和
    52 const md5 = crypto.createHash('md5').update(fs.readFileSync(path.join(STAGE, 'app.tgz'))).digest('hex');
    53 let manifest = fs.readFileSync(path.join(PKG_DIR, 'manifest'), 'utf8');
    54 manifest = manifest.replace(/^checksum\s*=.*$/m, `checksum=${md5}`);
    55 fs.writeFileSync(path.join(STAGE, 'manifest'), manifest);
    56 
    57 // 4. 复制其余打包文件（cmd / config / wizard / 图标 / 健康检查）
    58 fs.cpSync(path.join(PKG_DIR, 'cmd'), path.join(STAGE, 'cmd'), { recursive: true });
    59 fs.cpSync(path.join(PKG_DIR, 'config'), path.join(STAGE, 'config'), { recursive: true });
    60 fs.cpSync(path.join(PKG_DIR, 'wizard'), path.join(STAGE, 'wizard'), { recursive: true });
    61 fs.copyFileSync(path.join(PKG_DIR, 'ICON.PNG'), path.join(STAGE, 'ICON.PNG'));
    62 fs.copyFileSync(path.join(PKG_DIR, 'ICON_256.PNG'), path.join(STAGE, 'ICON_256.PNG'));
    63 fs.copyFileSync(path.join(PKG_DIR, 'health.json'), path.join(STAGE, 'health.json'));
    64 
    65 // 5. 打包 .fpk（使用 Python 确保纯净归档路径与 +x 脚本权限）
    66 console.log('>> 打包 .fpk ...');
    67 fs.mkdirSync(OUT_DIR, { recursive: true });
    68 rm(FPK);
    69 sh(`python3 -c '
    70 import os, tarfile
    71 stage = "${STAGE}"
    72 fpk_out = "${FPK}"
    73 with tarfile.open(fpk_out, "w:gz") as tar:
    74     for root, dirs, files in os.walk(stage):
    75         for name in dirs:
    76             fp = os.path.join(root, name)
    77             rel = os.path.relpath(fp, stage)
    78             ti = tar.gettarinfo(fp, arcname=rel)
    79             ti.mode = 0o755
    80             ti.uid = ti.gid = 0
    81             tar.addfile(ti)
    82         for name in files:
    83             fp = os.path.join(root, name)
    84             rel = os.path.relpath(fp, stage)
    85             ti = tar.gettarinfo(fp, arcname=rel)
    86             ti.mode = 0o755 if "cmd/" in rel or rel.endswith(".sh") else 0o644
    87             ti.uid = ti.gid = 0
    88             with open(fp, "rb") as f:
    89                 tar.addfile(ti, f)
    90 '`, STAGE);
    91 rm(STAGE);
    92 console.log(`>> 完成: ${FPK} (${fs.statSync(FPK).size} bytes)`);
    93

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""打包 fnOS IP台账管理 .fpk 安装包（CGI/server.py 版本，1.0.9）"""
import os, tarfile, hashlib, json, shutil
from PIL import Image

ROOT_DIR = "/workspace/app-e48he9p4w001"
BUILD_DIR = f"{ROOT_DIR}/packaging/build_v109"
OUTPUT_FPK = f"{ROOT_DIR}/packaging/ipam-1.0.9.fpk"
OUTPUT_FPK_ALIAS = f"{ROOT_DIR}/packaging/ipam.fpk"

if os.path.exists(BUILD_DIR):
    shutil.rmtree(BUILD_DIR)
os.makedirs(BUILD_DIR, exist_ok=True)

src_img = Image.open(f"{ROOT_DIR}/tasks/IP.png").convert("RGBA")
img_64 = src_img.resize((64, 64), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
img_256 = src_img.resize((256, 256), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)

APP_STAGE = f"{BUILD_DIR}/app_stage"
os.makedirs(f"{APP_STAGE}/www", exist_ok=True)
os.makedirs(f"{APP_STAGE}/ui/images", exist_ok=True)

shutil.copytree(f"{ROOT_DIR}/dist", f"{APP_STAGE}/www", dirs_exist_ok=True)

with open(f"{APP_STAGE}/www/index.html", "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    html = f.read()
html = html.replace('href="/assets/', 'href="./assets/').replace('src="/assets/', 'src="./assets/')
html = html.replace('href="/favicon', 'href="./favicon').replace('src="/logo', 'src="./logo')
with open(f"{APP_STAGE}/www/index.html", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(html)

img_64.save(f"{APP_STAGE}/ui/images/icon_64.png")
img_256.save(f"{APP_STAGE}/ui/images/icon_256.png")

ui_config = {
    ".url": {
        "ipam.main": {
            "title": "IP台账管理",
            "icon": "images/icon_{0}.png",
            "type": "iframe",
            "protocol": "http",
            "port": "28090",
            "url": "/",
            "allUsers": True
        }
    }
}
with open(f"{APP_STAGE}/ui/config", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(ui_config, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)

server_py = '''#!/usr/bin/env python3
import sys, os
from http.server import SimpleHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
port = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 28090
web_dir = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)) + "/www"
class SPARequestHandler(SimpleHTTPRequestHandler):
    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(*args, directory=web_dir, **kwargs)
    def do_GET(self):
        path = self.path.split('?')[0]
        full_path = os.path.join(web_dir, path.lstrip('/'))
        if not os.path.exists(full_path) and not path.startswith('/assets/'):
            self.path = '/index.html'
        return super().do_GET()
    def end_headers(self):
        self.send_header('Cache-Control', 'no-cache')
        super().end_headers()
if __name__ == '__main__':
    HTTPServer(('0.0.0.0', port), SPARequestHandler).serve_forever()
'''
with open(f"{APP_STAGE}/server.py", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(server_py)
os.chmod(f"{APP_STAGE}/server.py", 0o755)

shutil.copy(f"{ROOT_DIR}/packaging/templates-cgi/app/ui/index.cgi", f"{APP_STAGE}/ui/index.cgi")
os.chmod(f"{APP_STAGE}/ui/index.cgi", 0o755)

APP_TGZ = f"{BUILD_DIR}/app.tgz"
with tarfile.open(APP_TGZ, "w:gz", format=tarfile.GNU_FORMAT) as tar:
    for entry in sorted(os.listdir(APP_STAGE)):
        tar.add(os.path.join(APP_STAGE, entry), arcname=entry)

with open(APP_TGZ, "rb") as f:
    app_md5 = hashlib.md5(f.read()).hexdigest()

FPK_STAGE = f"{BUILD_DIR}/fpk_stage"
os.makedirs(f"{FPK_STAGE}/config", exist_ok=True)
os.makedirs(f"{FPK_STAGE}/cmd", exist_ok=True)
os.makedirs(f"{FPK_STAGE}/wizard", exist_ok=True)

with open(f"{FPK_STAGE}/wizard/.keep", "w") as f:
    f.write("")

health_data = {"protocol": "http", "port": 28090, "path": "/", "interval": 10, "timeout": 5}
with open(f"{FPK_STAGE}/health.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(health_data, f, indent=2)

shutil.copy(APP_TGZ, f"{FPK_STAGE}/app.tgz")
img_64.save(f"{FPK_STAGE}/ICON.PNG")
img_256.save(f"{FPK_STAGE}/ICON_256.PNG")

manifest = f"""appname = ipam
version = 1.0.9
display_name = IP台账管理
desc = 面向飞牛 OS 的 IP 台账管理系统：多用户权限协同与角色分配、IP规划、子网划分、VLAN、设备资产、路由器地址表导入与本地备份。本地部署，数据云端持久保存。
platform = all
source = thirdparty
maintainer = 梦非常浪漫
distributor = 梦非常浪漫
desktop_uidir = ui
desktop_applaunchname = ipam.main
service_port = 28090
checkport = true
ctl_stop = true
checksum = {app_md5}
"""
with open(f"{FPK_STAGE}/manifest", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(manifest)

with open(f"{FPK_STAGE}/config/privilege", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump({"defaults": {"run-as": "package"}, "username": "ipam", "groupname": "ipam"}, f, indent=2)
with open(f"{FPK_STAGE}/config/resource", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write("{}\n")

cmd_main = """#!/bin/bash
PORT=${TRIM_SERVICE_PORT:-28090}
PID_FILE="${TRIM_PKGVAR:-/tmp}/ipam_app.pid"
LOG_FILE="${TRIM_PKGVAR:-/tmp}/ipam_app.log"
APP_DIR="${TRIM_APPDEST:-/var/apps/ipam/target}"
WWW_DIR="${APP_DIR}/www"
if [ ! -d "$WWW_DIR" ]; then
    WWW_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/../app/www" 2>/dev/null && pwd)"
fi
start_service() {
    if is_running; then return 0; fi
    mkdir -p "$(dirname "$PID_FILE")" "$(dirname "$LOG_FILE")"
    if [ -f "${APP_DIR}/server.py" ] && command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        python3 "${APP_DIR}/server.py" "${PORT}" "${WWW_DIR}" >> "${LOG_FILE}" 2>&1 &
        echo $! > "${PID_FILE}"
    elif command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        python3 -m http.server "${PORT}" --directory "${WWW_DIR}" >> "${LOG_FILE}" 2>&1 &
        echo $! > "${PID_FILE}"
    fi
    sleep 0.5
}
stop_service() {
    if [ -f "${PID_FILE}" ]; then kill $(cat "${PID_FILE}") 2>/dev/null; rm -f "${PID_FILE}"; fi
    pkill -f "server.py ${PORT}" 2>/dev/null || true
}
is_running() {
    if [ -f "${PID_FILE}" ] && kill -0 $(cat "${PID_FILE}") 2>/dev/null; then return 0; fi
    if command -v ss >/dev/null 2>&1 && ss -tulpn | grep -q ":${PORT} "; then return 0; fi
    return 1
}
case "$1" in
    start) start_service ;;
    stop) stop_service ;;
    status) is_running && exit 0 || exit 1 ;;
    restart) stop_service; start_service ;;
    *) exit 1 ;;
esac
"""
with open(f"{FPK_STAGE}/cmd/main", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(cmd_main)
os.chmod(f"{FPK_STAGE}/cmd/main", 0o755)

for s in ["install_init","install_callback","upgrade_init","upgrade_callback","uninstall_init","uninstall_callback","config_init","config_callback"]:
    p = f"{FPK_STAGE}/cmd/{s}"
    with open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("#!/bin/bash\nexit 0\n")
    os.chmod(p, 0o755)

with tarfile.open(OUTPUT_FPK, "w:gz", format=tarfile.GNU_FORMAT) as tar:
    for entry in sorted(os.listdir(FPK_STAGE)):
        tar.add(os.path.join(FPK_STAGE, entry), arcname=entry)

shutil.copy(OUTPUT_FPK, OUTPUT_FPK_ALIAS)
print(f"OK FPK 生成成功: {OUTPUT_FPK} ({os.path.getsize(OUTPUT_FPK)} bytes)")
```

// 飞牛经典牛头 Logo SVG
function FnosBullLogo({ className = 'w-16 h-16' }: { className?: string }) {

export default function LoginPage() {
  const { login, loginWithNas, nasInfo } = useAuth();
  
  // 模式：默认为 'nas_auth'（飞牛系统授权卡片，完美还原用户截图），支持切换为 'password'（其他账号密码）
  const [authMode, setAuthMode] = useState<'nas_auth' | 'password'>('nas_auth');
  
  // 飞牛系统授权身份信息
  const deviceName = nasInfo?.hostname || 'PGHY-NASII';
  const detectedUser = nasInfo?.username || 'hyadmin';
  const [nasUser, setNasUser] = useState(detectedUser);

  // 账号密码表单状态
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [rememberMe, setRememberMe] = useState(true);
  
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [nasAuthLoading, setNasAuthLoading] = useState(false);

  // 点击飞牛系统「授权」按钮
  const handleNasAuthConfirm = async () => {
    setNasAuthLoading(true);
    const targetUser = nasUser.trim() || 'hyadmin';
    const res = await loginWithNas(targetUser);
    setNasAuthLoading(false);
    if (!res.ok) {
      toast.error(res.error ?? '飞牛系统授权登录失败');
      return;
    }
    toast.success(`飞牛系统账号 [${targetUser}] 授权成功，欢迎使用！`);
  };

  // 提交其他账号密码登录
  const handlePasswordSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!username.trim() || !password) {
      toast.error('请输入用户名和密码');
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    const res = await login(username, password);
    setLoading(false);
    if (!res.ok) {
      toast.error(res.error ?? '登录失败');
      return;
    }
    toast.success('登录成功');
  };

  return (
    <div className="relative flex min-h-screen w-full items-center justify-center p-4 text-white select-none overflow-hidden font-sans">
      {/* 飞牛标志性绚丽极光流体背景壁纸 (100% 还原用户截图彩虹流体渐变背景) */}
      <div 
        className="absolute inset-0 z-0 bg-cover bg-center"
        style={{
          background: `
            radial-gradient(circle at 10% 20%, rgba(255, 140, 50, 0.85) 0%, transparent 45%),
            radial-gradient(circle at 90% 15%, rgba(240, 90, 140, 0.8) 0%, transparent 40%),
            radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%, rgba(130, 80, 220, 0.75) 0%, transparent 55%),
            radial-gradient(circle at 80% 85%, rgba(40, 180, 220, 0.85) 0%, transparent 50%),
            radial-gradient(circle at 20% 90%, rgba(30, 200, 160, 0.85) 0%, transparent 45%),
            linear-gradient(135deg, #f7a044 0%, #b85098 35%, #4f46e5 70%, #06b6d4 100%)
          `
        }}
      />
      {/* 微妙遮罩提升磨砂对比度 */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 z-0 bg-black/15 backdrop-blur-[2px]" />

      {/* 模式 1：飞牛系统授权主卡片 (完全像素级还原用户截图 Image1) */}
      {authMode === 'nas_auth' ? (
        <div className="relative z-10 w-full max-w-[340px] rounded-3xl border border-white/25 bg-white/15 dark:bg-black/25 p-7 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-2xl text-center text-white animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200">
          {/* 飞牛经典白色牛头 LOGO */}
          <div className="mx-auto mb-2 flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center text-white drop-shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(255,255,255,0.3)]">
            <FnosBullLogo className="w-14 h-14" />
          </div>

          {/* 飞牛设备名称 */}
          <h2 className="text-base font-semibold tracking-wide text-white/95 mb-5 drop-shadow-sm">
            {deviceName}
          </h2>

          {/* 飞牛系统当前账户胶囊展示条 */}
          <div className="mx-auto mb-5 flex items-center justify-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-white/20 bg-white/20 dark:bg-white/10 px-4 py-3 shadow-inner backdrop-blur-md">
            {/* 头像圆徽 */}
            <div className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-blue-600 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-md shadow-blue-600/30">
              {(nasUser || 'h').charAt(0).toLowerCase()}
            </div>
            {/* 账户名 */}
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-white tracking-wider">
              {nasUser || 'hyadmin'}
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* 请求授权文案 */}
          <p className="mb-6 text-xs leading-relaxed text-white/90 px-1 text-pretty drop-shadow-sm">
            “IP台账管理” 正在请求你的 NAS 账号登录，是否确认授权？
          </p>

          {/* 授权核心按钮 */}
          <Button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleNasAuthConfirm}
            disabled={nasAuthLoading}
            className="w-full h-11 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-medium text-sm shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/40 transition-all active:scale-[0.98]"
          >
            {nasAuthLoading ? '正在授权...' : '授 权'}
          </Button>

          {/* 切换使用其他账号 */}
          <div className="mt-4 pt-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setAuthMode('password')}
              className="text-xs text-amber-200 hover:text-white transition-colors drop-shadow-sm font-normal py-1 px-3"
            >
              使用其他账号
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* 模式 2：其他账号密码登录表单 */
        <div className="relative z-10 w-full max-w-[380px] rounded-3xl border border-white/20 bg-slate-900/80 p-7 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-2xl text-white animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200">
          <div className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setAuthMode('nas_auth')}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span>返回飞牛授权</span>
            </button>
            <span className="text-xs text-blue-400 font-medium">账号密码登录</span>
          </div>

          <div className="mb-5 text-center">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
              <KeyRound className="h-5 w-5 text-blue-400" />
              <span>登录 IP台账管理</span>
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">{deviceName}</p>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handlePasswordSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="username" className="text-xs font-normal text-slate-300">
                用户名
              </Label>
              <Input
                id="username"
                value={username}
                onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="请输入用户名"
                autoComplete="username"
                className="h-10 border-white/10 bg-black/40 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:border-blue-500 rounded-lg"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="password" className="text-xs font-normal text-slate-300">
                密码
              </Label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Input
                  id="password"
                  type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="请输入密码"
                  autoComplete="current-password"
                  className="h-10 border-white/10 bg-black/40 pr-10 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:border-blue-500 rounded-lg"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                  className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
                >
                  {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-1 text-xs text-slate-400">
              <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none">
                <Checkbox
                  checked={rememberMe}
                  onCheckedChange={(v) => setRememberMe(!!v)}
                  className="border-white/20 data-[state=checked]:bg-blue-600"
                />
                <span>记住账号</span>
              </label>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => toast.info('请在飞牛系统控制面板或联系管理员重置')}
                className="hover:text-slate-200"
              >
                忘记密码？
              </button>
            </div>

            <Button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full h-10 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-medium text-sm shadow-md transition-all mt-2"
            >
              {loading ? '登录中...' : '登录'}
            </Button>
          </form>

          <div className="mt-5 border-t border-white/10 pt-3 text-center">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setAuthMode('nas_auth')}
              className="text-xs text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 transition-colors"
            >
              ← 使用飞牛系统授权一键登录
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 底部 SQLite 存储说明 */}
      <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-0 right-0 text-center pointer-events-none z-10">
        <p className="text-[11px] text-white/70 drop-shadow flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
          <ShieldCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-300" />
          <span>本地飞牛 NAS SQLite 持久化 · 离线安全运行</span>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

cmd_main = """#!/bin/bash
PORT=${TRIM_SERVICE_PORT:-28090}
PID_FILE="${TRIM_PKGVAR:-/tmp}/ipam_app.pid"
LOG_FILE="${TRIM_PKGVAR:-/tmp}/ipam_app.log"
APP_DIR="${TRIM_APPDEST:-/var/apps/ipam/target}"
WWW_DIR="${APP_DIR}/www"

start_service() {
    if is_running; then return 0; fi
    mkdir -p "$(dirname "$PID_FILE")" "$(dirname "$LOG_FILE")" "${TRIM_PKGVAR:-/tmp}"
    if [ -f "${APP_DIR}/server.py" ] && command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        export TRIM_PKGVAR="${TRIM_PKGVAR:-/tmp}"
        export TRIM_APPDEST="${APP_DIR}"
        python3 "${APP_DIR}/server.py" "${PORT}" "${WWW_DIR}" >> "${LOG_FILE}" 2>&1 &
        echo $! > "${PID_FILE}"
    fi
    sleep 0.5
}
stop_service() {
    if [ -f "${PID_FILE}" ]; then kill $(cat "${PID_FILE}") 2>/dev/null; rm -f "${PID_FILE}"; fi
    pkill -f "server.py ${PORT}" 2>/dev/null || true
    rm -f "${APP_DIR}/app.sock"
}
is_running() {
    if [ -f "${PID_FILE}" ] && kill -0 $(cat "${PID_FILE}") 2>/dev/null; then return 0; fi
    if command -v ss >/dev/null 2>&1 && ss -tulpn 2>/dev/null | grep -q ":${PORT} "; then return 0; fi
    return 1
}
case "$1" in
    start) start_service ;;
    stop) stop_service ;;
    status) is_running && exit 0 || exit 1 ;;
    restart) stop_service; start_service ;;
    *) exit 1 ;;
esac
exit 0
"""
with open(f"{FPK_STAGE}/cmd/main", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(cmd_main)
os.chmod(f"{FPK_STAGE}/cmd/main", 0o755)

if [ -f "$PID_FILE" ]; then

    rm -f "$PID_FILE"

if [ -n "$TRIM_PKGVAR" ] && [ -d "$TRIM_PKGVAR" ]; then
    rm -rf "${TRIM_PKGVAR:?}"/* 2>/dev/null || true
    rm -rf "${TRIM_PKGVAR:?}"/.* 2>/dev/null || true
fi
if [ -n "$TRIM_PKGETC" ] && [ -d "$TRIM_PKGETC" ]; then

fi
if [ -n "$TRIM_APPTMP" ] && [ -d "$TRIM_APPTMP" ]; then
    rm -rf "${TRIM_APPTMP:?}"/* 2>/dev/null || true
fi
exit 0
"""
with open(f"{FPK_STAGE}/cmd/uninstall_init", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
